  <div class="clearfix container-xl tmp-px-5 tmp-mt-4 page" data-hpc="true" id="wiki-wrapper">
    <div class="d-flex flex-column flex-md-row gh-header">
      <h1 class="flex-auto min-width-0 mb-2 mb-md-0 mr-0 mr-md-2 gh-header-title">Usage</h1>

        <div class="mt-0 mt-lg-1 flex-shrink-0 gh-header-actions">
          <a href="#wiki-pages-box" class="d-md-none ">Jump to bottom</a>

      <div class="mt-2 mt-md-1 tmp-pb-3 gh-header-meta">
        Johan Haleby edited this page <relative-time datetime="2026-10-23T11:56:46Z" class="no-wrap">Oct 23, 2026</relative-time>
        &middot;

    <div id="wiki-content" class="tmp-mt-4">
      <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout Layout--flowRow-until-md Layout--sidebarPosition-end Layout--sidebarPosition-flowRow-end">
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout-main">          <div id="wiki-body" class="gollum-markdown-content">
              <div class="markdown-body">
                <p>Note that if you're using version 1.9.0 or earlier please refer to the <a href="Usage_Legacy">legacy</a> documentation.</p>
<p>REST Assured is a Java DSL for simplifying testing of REST based services built on top of HTTP Builder. It supports POST, GET, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS, PATCH and HEAD requests and can be used to validate and verify the response of these requests.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Contents</h1><a id="user-content-contents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contents" href="#contents"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Static imports</h2><a id="user-content-static-imports" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Static imports" href="#static-imports"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>In order to use REST assured effectively it's recommended to statically import methods from the following classes:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="io.restassured.RestAssured.*
io.restassured.matcher.RestAssuredMatchers.*
org.hamcrest.Matchers.*"><pre><span class="pl-smi">io</span>.<span class="pl-smi">restassured</span>.<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.*
<span class="pl-smi">io</span>.<span class="pl-smi">restassured</span>.<span class="pl-smi">matcher</span>.<span class="pl-smi">RestAssuredMatchers</span>.*
<span class="pl-smi">org</span>.<span class="pl-smi">hamcrest</span>.<span class="pl-smi">Matchers</span>.*</pre></div>
<p>If you want to use <a href="http://json-schema.org/" rel="nofollow">Json Schema</a> validation you should also statically import these methods:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="io.restassured.module.jsv.JsonSchemaValidator.*"><pre><span class="pl-smi">io</span>.<span class="pl-smi">restassured</span>.<span class="pl-smi">module</span>.<span class="pl-smi">jsv</span>.<span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaValidator</span>.*</pre></div>
<p>Refer to <a href="#json-schema-validation">Json Schema Validation</a> section for more info.</p>
<p>If you're using Spring MVC you can use the <a href="#spring-mock-mvc-module">spring-mock-mvc</a> module to unit test your Spring Controllers using the Rest Assured DSL. To do this statically import the methods from <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/spring-mock-mvc/6.3.0/io/restassured/module/mockmvc/RestAssuredMockMvc.html" rel="nofollow">RestAssuredMockMvc</a> <em>instead</em> of importing the methods from <code>io.restassured.RestAssured</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="io.restassured.module.mockmvc.RestAssuredMockMvc.*"><pre><span class="pl-smi">io</span>.<span class="pl-smi">restassured</span>.<span class="pl-smi">module</span>.<span class="pl-smi">mockmvc</span>.<span class="pl-smi">RestAssuredMockMvc</span>.*</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Examples</h1><a id="user-content-examples" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Examples" href="#examples"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Example 1 - JSON</h2><a id="user-content-example-1---json" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 1 - JSON" href="#example-1---json"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Assume that the GET request (to <a href="http://localhost:8080/lotto" rel="nofollow">http://localhost:8080/lotto</a>) returns JSON as:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
&quot;lotto&quot;:{
 &quot;lottoId&quot;:5,
 &quot;winning-numbers&quot;:[2,45,34,23,7,5,3],
 &quot;winners&quot;:[{
   &quot;winnerId&quot;:23,
   &quot;numbers&quot;:[2,45,34,23,3,5]
 },{
   &quot;winnerId&quot;:54,
   &quot;numbers&quot;:[52,3,12,11,18,22]
 }]
}
}"><pre><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
<span class="pl-s">"lotto"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">{</span>
 <span class="pl-s">"lottoId"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">5</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
 <span class="pl-s">"winning-numbers"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">45</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">34</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">23</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">7</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
 <span class="pl-s">"winners"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
   <span class="pl-s">"winnerId"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">23</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
   <span class="pl-s">"numbers"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">45</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">34</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">23</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
 <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
   <span class="pl-s">"winnerId"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">54</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
   <span class="pl-s">"numbers"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">52</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">12</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">11</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">18</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span><span class="pl-c1">22</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
 <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p>REST assured can then help you to easily make the GET request and verify the response. E.g. if you want to verify that lottoId is equal to 5 you can do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).then().body(&quot;lotto.lottoId&quot;, equalTo(5));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.lottoId"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">5</span>));</pre></div>
<p>or perhaps you want to check that the winnerId's are 23 and 54:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).then().body(&quot;lotto.winners.winnerId&quot;, hasItems(23, 54));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.winners.winnerId"</span>, <span class="pl-en">hasItems</span>(<span class="pl-c1">23</span>, <span class="pl-c1">54</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Note: <code>equalTo</code> and <code>hasItems</code> are Hamcrest matchers which you should statically import from <code>org.hamcrest.Matchers</code>.</p>
<p>Note that the "json path" syntax uses <a href="http://groovy-lang.org/processing-xml.html#_gpath" rel="nofollow">Groovy's GPath</a> notation and is not to be confused with Jayway's <a href="https://github.com/jayway/JsonPath">JsonPath</a> syntax.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Returning floats and doubles as BigDecimal</h3><a id="user-content-returning-floats-and-doubles-as-bigdecimal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Returning floats and doubles as BigDecimal" href="#returning-floats-and-doubles-as-bigdecimal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can configure Rest Assured and JsonPath to return BigDecimal's instead of float and double for Json Numbers. For example consider the following JSON document:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{

    &quot;price&quot;:12.12 

}"><pre><span class="pl-kos">{</span>

    <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">12.12</span> 

<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p>By default  you validate that price is equal to 12.12 as a float like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/price&quot;).then().body(&quot;price&quot;, is(12.12f));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/price"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"price"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(<span class="pl-c1">12.12f</span>));</pre></div>
<p>but if you like you can configure REST Assured to use a JsonConfig that returns all Json numbers as BigDecimal:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(RestAssured.config().jsonConfig(jsonConfig().numberReturnType(BIG_DECIMAL))).
when().
        get(&quot;/price&quot;).
then().
        body(&quot;price&quot;, is(new BigDecimal(12.12));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">jsonConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">jsonConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">numberReturnType</span>(<span class="pl-c1">BIG_DECIMAL</span>))).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/price"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"price"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">BigDecimal</span>(<span class="pl-c1">12.12</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">JSON Schema validation</h3><a id="user-content-json-schema-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JSON Schema validation" href="#json-schema-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>From version <code>2.1.0</code> REST Assured has support for <a href="http://json-schema.org/" rel="nofollow">Json Schema</a> validation. For example given the following schema located in the classpath as <code>products-schema.json</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
    &quot;$schema&quot;: &quot;http://json-schema.org/draft-04/schema#&quot;,
    &quot;title&quot;: &quot;Product set&quot;,
    &quot;type&quot;: &quot;array&quot;,
    &quot;items&quot;: {
        &quot;title&quot;: &quot;Product&quot;,
        &quot;type&quot;: &quot;object&quot;,
        &quot;properties&quot;: {
            &quot;id&quot;: {
                &quot;description&quot;: &quot;The unique identifier for a product&quot;,
                &quot;type&quot;: &quot;number&quot;
            },
            &quot;name&quot;: {
                &quot;type&quot;: &quot;string&quot;
            },
            &quot;price&quot;: {
                &quot;type&quot;: &quot;number&quot;,
                &quot;minimum&quot;: 0,
                &quot;exclusiveMinimum&quot;: true
            },
            &quot;tags&quot;: {
                &quot;type&quot;: &quot;array&quot;,
                &quot;items&quot;: {
                    &quot;type&quot;: &quot;string&quot;
                },
                &quot;minItems&quot;: 1,
                &quot;uniqueItems&quot;: true
            },
            &quot;dimensions&quot;: {
                &quot;type&quot;: &quot;object&quot;,
                &quot;properties&quot;: {
                    &quot;length&quot;: {&quot;type&quot;: &quot;number&quot;},
                    &quot;width&quot;: {&quot;type&quot;: &quot;number&quot;},
                    &quot;height&quot;: {&quot;type&quot;: &quot;number&quot;}
                },
                &quot;required&quot;: [&quot;length&quot;, &quot;width&quot;, &quot;height&quot;]
            },
            &quot;warehouseLocation&quot;: {
                &quot;description&quot;: &quot;Coordinates of the warehouse with the product&quot;,
                &quot;$ref&quot;: &quot;http://json-schema.org/geo&quot;
            }
        },
        &quot;required&quot;: [&quot;id&quot;, &quot;name&quot;, &quot;price&quot;]
    }
}"><pre><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"$schema"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"http://json-schema.org/draft-04/schema#"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Product set"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"array"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-s">"items"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
        <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Product"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"object"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-s">"properties"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"description"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"The unique identifier for a product"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"number"</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"name"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"string"</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"number"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"minimum"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"exclusiveMinimum"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"tags"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"array"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"items"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                    <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"string"</span>
                <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"minItems"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"uniqueItems"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"dimensions"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"object"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"properties"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                    <span class="pl-s">"length"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"number"</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                    <span class="pl-s">"width"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"number"</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                    <span class="pl-s">"height"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"number"</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
                <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"required"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"length"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"width"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"height"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"warehouseLocation"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"description"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Coordinates of the warehouse with the product"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"$ref"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"http://json-schema.org/geo"</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-s">"required"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"id"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"name"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"price"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p>you can validate that a resource (<code>/products</code>) conforms with the schema:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/products&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;products-schema.json&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/products"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"products-schema.json"</span>));</pre></div>
<p><code>matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</code> is statically imported from <code>io.restassured.module.jsv.JsonSchemaValidator</code> and it's recommended to statically import all methods from this class. However in order to use it you need to depend on the <code>json-schema-validator</code> module by either <a href="http://dl.bintray.com/johanhaleby/generic/json-schema-validator-6.3.0-dist.zip" rel="nofollow">downloading</a> it from the download page or add the following dependency from Maven:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;dependency&gt;
    &lt;groupId&gt;io.rest-assured&lt;/groupId&gt;
    &lt;artifactId&gt;json-schema-validator&lt;/artifactId&gt;
    &lt;version&gt;6.3.0&lt;/version&gt;
&lt;/dependency&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">groupId</span>&gt;io.rest-assured&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">groupId</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;json-schema-validator&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">version</span>&gt;6.3.0&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">version</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">JSON Schema Validation Settings</h3><a id="user-content-json-schema-validation-settings" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JSON Schema Validation Settings" href="#json-schema-validation-settings"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>REST Assured's <code>json-schema-validator</code> module uses Francis Galiegue's <a href="https://github.com/fge/json-schema-validator">json-schema-validator</a> (<code>fge</code>) library to perform validation. If you need to configure the underlying <code>fge</code> library you can for example do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Given
JsonSchemaFactory jsonSchemaFactory = JsonSchemaFactory.newBuilder().setValidationConfiguration(ValidationConfiguration.newBuilder().setDefaultVersion(DRAFTV4).freeze()).freeze();

// When
get(&quot;/products&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;products-schema.json&quot;).using(jsonSchemaFactory));"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Given</span>
<span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaFactory</span> <span class="pl-s1">jsonSchemaFactory</span> = <span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaFactory</span>.<span class="pl-en">newBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setValidationConfiguration</span>(<span class="pl-smi">ValidationConfiguration</span>.<span class="pl-en">newBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setDefaultVersion</span>(<span class="pl-c1">DRAFTV4</span>).<span class="pl-en">freeze</span>()).<span class="pl-en">freeze</span>();

<span class="pl-c">// When</span>
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/products"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"products-schema.json"</span>).<span class="pl-en">using</span>(<span class="pl-s1">jsonSchemaFactory</span>));</pre></div>
<p>The <code>using</code> method allows you to pass in a <code>jsonSchemaFactory</code> instance that REST Assured will use during validation. This allows fine-grained configuration for the validation.</p>
<p>The <code>fge</code> library also allows the validation to be <code>checked</code> or <code>unchecked</code>. By default REST Assured uses <code>checked</code> validation but if you want to change this you can supply an instance of <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/json-schema-validator/6.3.0/io/restassured/module/jsv/JsonSchemaValidatorSettings.html" rel="nofollow">JsonSchemaValidatorSettings</a> to the matcher. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/products&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;products-schema.json&quot;).using(settings().with().checkedValidation(false)));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/products"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"products-schema.json"</span>).<span class="pl-en">using</span>(<span class="pl-en">settings</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">checkedValidation</span>(<span class="pl-c1">false</span>)));</pre></div>
<p>Where the <code>settings</code> method is statically imported from the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/json-schema-validator/6.3.0/io/restassured/module/jsv/JsonSchemaValidatorSettings.html" rel="nofollow">JsonSchemaValidatorSettings</a> class.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Choosing the JSON Schema version</h3><a id="user-content-choosing-the-json-schema-version" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Choosing the JSON Schema version" href="#choosing-the-json-schema-version"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As of REST Assured 6.1.0 the json-schema-validator module supports JSON Schema draft-06, draft-07, 2019-09 and 2020-12, alongside draft-03 and draft-04. You don't need to change anything to use them. The module reads the <code>$schema</code> keyword in your schema and validates with that draft:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;$schema&quot;: &quot;https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema&quot;,
  &quot;type&quot;: &quot;object&quot;,
  &quot;properties&quot;: {
    &quot;status&quot;: { &quot;const&quot;: &quot;active&quot; }
  },
  &quot;required&quot;: [&quot;status&quot;]
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"$schema"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"type"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>object<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"properties"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"status"</span>: { <span class="pl-ent">"const"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>active<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> }
  },
  <span class="pl-ent">"required"</span>: [<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>status<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>]
}</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/accounts/1&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;account-schema.json&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/accounts/1"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"account-schema.json"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Schemas that declare draft-03 or draft-04, or don't declare <code>$schema</code> at all, are validated as before. If your schemas leave out <code>$schema</code> and you want a newer draft, say which one:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/accounts/1&quot;).then().body(matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;account-schema.json&quot;).using(JsonSchemaVersion.DRAFT_2020_12));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/accounts/1"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"account-schema.json"</span>).<span class="pl-en">using</span>(<span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaVersion</span>.<span class="pl-c1">DRAFT_2020_12</span>));</pre></div>
<p>or for all validations:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JsonSchemaValidator.settings = settings().with().schemaVersion(JsonSchemaVersion.DRAFT_7);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaValidator</span>.<span class="pl-s1">settings</span> = <span class="pl-en">settings</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">schemaVersion</span>(<span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaVersion</span>.<span class="pl-c1">DRAFT_7</span>);</pre></div>
<p>A <code>$schema</code> declared in the schema always takes precedence over the configured version.</p>
<p>A few things to know:</p>
<ul>
<li>If a schema already declared draft-06 or later, older REST Assured versions quietly validated it with draft-04 rules and ignored newer keywords such as <code>const</code> and <code>if</code>/<code>then</code>/<code>else</code>. Those keywords are now checked, so a test that passed before can start failing because the response really doesn't match the schema.</li>
<li>A custom <code>JsonSchemaFactory</code> and <code>checkedValidation</code> only apply to draft-03 and draft-04 schemas.</li>
<li>In 2019-09 and 2020-12, <code>format</code> (for example <code>"format": "email"</code>) is an annotation and isn't validated, as those specifications require.</li>
<li>Relative <code>$ref</code>s work for schemas loaded from the classpath, a <code>File</code>, a URL or a URI. A schema passed as a String, InputStream or Reader has no location to resolve them against.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Json Schema Validation with static configuration</h3><a id="user-content-json-schema-validation-with-static-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Json Schema Validation with static configuration" href="#json-schema-validation-with-static-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Now imagine that you always want to use <code>unchecked</code> validation as well as setting the default json schema version to version 3. Instead of supplying this to all matchers throughout your code you can define it statically. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JsonSchemaValidator.settings = settings().with().jsonSchemaFactory(
        JsonSchemaFactory.newBuilder().setValidationConfiguration(ValidationConfiguration.newBuilder().setDefaultVersion(DRAFTV3).freeze()).freeze()).
        and().with().checkedValidation(false);

get(&quot;/products&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;products-schema.json&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaValidator</span>.<span class="pl-s1">settings</span> = <span class="pl-en">settings</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">jsonSchemaFactory</span>(
        <span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaFactory</span>.<span class="pl-en">newBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setValidationConfiguration</span>(<span class="pl-smi">ValidationConfiguration</span>.<span class="pl-en">newBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setDefaultVersion</span>(<span class="pl-c1">DRAFTV3</span>).<span class="pl-en">freeze</span>()).<span class="pl-en">freeze</span>()).
        <span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">checkedValidation</span>(<span class="pl-c1">false</span>);

<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/products"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"products-schema.json"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Now any <code>matcher</code> method imported from <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/json-schema-validator/6.3.0/io/restassured/module/jsv/JsonSchemaValidatorSettings.html" rel="nofollow">JsonSchemaValidator</a> will use <code>DRAFTV3</code> as default version and unchecked validation.</p>
<p>To reset the <code>JsonSchemaValidator</code> to its default settings simply call the <code>reset</code> method:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JsonSchemaValidator.reset();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaValidator</span>.<span class="pl-en">reset</span>();</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Json Schema Validation without REST Assured</h3><a id="user-content-json-schema-validation-without-rest-assured" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Json Schema Validation without REST Assured" href="#json-schema-validation-without-rest-assured"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also use the <code>json-schema-validator</code> module without depending on REST Assured. As long as you have a JSON document represented as a <code>String</code> you can do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import org.junit.Test;
import static io.restassured.module.jsv.JsonSchemaValidator.matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath;
import static org.hamcrest.MatcherAssert.assertThat;
 
public class JsonSchemaValidatorWithoutRestAssuredTest {
 
 
    @Test public void
    validates_schema_in_classpath() {
        // Given
        String json = ... // Greeting response
 
        // Then
        assertThat(json, matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath(&quot;greeting-schema.json&quot;));
    }
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">org</span>.<span class="pl-s1">junit</span>.<span class="pl-s1">Test</span>;
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-k">static</span> <span class="pl-s1">io</span>.<span class="pl-s1">restassured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">module</span>.<span class="pl-s1">jsv</span>.<span class="pl-s1">JsonSchemaValidator</span>.<span class="pl-s1">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>;
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-k">static</span> <span class="pl-s1">org</span>.<span class="pl-s1">hamcrest</span>.<span class="pl-s1">MatcherAssert</span>.<span class="pl-s1">assertThat</span>;
 
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">JsonSchemaValidatorWithoutRestAssuredTest</span> {
 
 
    <span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Test</span> <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">void</span>
    <span class="pl-en">validates_schema_in_classpath</span>() {
        <span class="pl-c">// Given</span>
        <span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">json</span> = ... <span class="pl-c">// Greeting response</span>
 
        <span class="pl-c">// Then</span>
        <span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">json</span>, <span class="pl-en">matchesJsonSchemaInClasspath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting-schema.json"</span>));
    }
}</pre></div>
<p>Refer to the <a href="GattingStarted">getting started</a> page for more info on this.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Anonymous JSON root validation</h3><a id="user-content-anonymous-json-root-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Anonymous JSON root validation" href="#anonymous-json-root-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>A JSON document doesn't necessarily need a named root attribute. This is for example valid JSON:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[1, 2, 3]"><pre><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">3</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span></pre></div>
<p>An anonymous JSON root can be verified by using <code>$</code> or an empty string as path. For example let's say that this JSON document is exposed from <code>http://localhost:8080/json</code> then we can validate it like this with REST Assured:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
     get(&quot;/json&quot;).
then().
     body(&quot;$&quot;, hasItems(1, 2, 3)); // An empty string &quot;&quot; would work as well"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
     <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/json"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
     <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"$"</span>, <span class="pl-en">hasItems</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>, <span class="pl-c1">2</span>, <span class="pl-c1">3</span>)); <span class="pl-c">// An empty string "" would work as well</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Example 2 - XML</h2><a id="user-content-example-2---xml" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 2 - XML" href="#example-2---xml"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>XML can be verified in a similar way. Imagine that a POST request to <code>http://localhost:8080/greetXML</code> returns:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;greeting&gt;
   &lt;firstName&gt;{params(&quot;firstName&quot;)}&lt;/firstName&gt;
   &lt;lastName&gt;{params(&quot;lastName&quot;)}&lt;/lastName&gt;
&lt;/greeting&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">greeting</span>&gt;
   &lt;<span class="pl-ent">firstName</span>&gt;{params("firstName")}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">firstName</span>&gt;
   &lt;<span class="pl-ent">lastName</span>&gt;{params("lastName")}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">lastName</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">greeting</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p>i.e. it sends back a greeting based on the firstName and lastName parameter sent in the request. You can easily perform and verify e.g. the firstName with REST assured:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
         parameters(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;, &quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).
when().
         post(&quot;/greetXML&quot;).
then().
         body(&quot;greeting.firstName&quot;, equalTo(&quot;John&quot;))."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">parameters</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting.firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>)).</pre></div>
<p>If you want to verify both firstName and lastName you may do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
         parameters(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;, &quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).
when().
         post(&quot;/greetXML&quot;).
then().
         body(&quot;greeting.firstName&quot;, equalTo(&quot;John&quot;)).
         body(&quot;greeting.lastName&quot;, equalTo(&quot;Doe&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">parameters</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting.firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting.lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>or a little shorter:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="with().parameters(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;, &quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).when().post(&quot;/greetXML&quot;).then().body(&quot;greeting.firstName&quot;, equalTo(&quot;John&quot;), &quot;greeting.lastName&quot;, equalTo(&quot;Doe&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">parameters</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting.firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>), <span class="pl-s">"greeting.lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>See <a href="http://groovy-lang.org/processing-xml.html#_gpath" rel="nofollow">this</a> link for more info about the syntax (it follows Groovy's <a href="http://groovy-lang.org/processing-xml.html#_gpath" rel="nofollow">GPath</a> syntax).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">XML namespaces</h3><a id="user-content-xml-namespaces" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XML namespaces" href="#xml-namespaces"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>To make body expectations take namespaces into account you need to declare the namespaces using the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/XmlConfig.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.config.XmlConfig</a>. For example let's say that a resource called <code>namespace-example</code> located at <code>http://localhost:8080</code> returns the following XML:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;foo xmlns:ns=&quot;http://localhost/&quot;&gt;
  &lt;bar&gt;sudo &lt;/bar&gt;
  &lt;ns:bar&gt;make me a sandwich!&lt;/ns:bar&gt;
&lt;/foo&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">foo</span> <span class="pl-e">xmlns</span><span class="pl-e">:</span><span class="pl-e">ns</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>http://localhost/<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>&gt;
  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">bar</span>&gt;sudo &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">bar</span>&gt;
  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">ns</span><span class="pl-ent">:</span><span class="pl-ent">bar</span>&gt;make me a sandwich!&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">ns</span><span class="pl-ent">:</span><span class="pl-ent">bar</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">foo</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p>You can then declare the <code>http://localhost/</code> uri and validate the response:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(RestAssured.config().xmlConfig(xmlConfig().declareNamespace(&quot;test&quot;, &quot;http://localhost/&quot;))).
when().
         get(&quot;/namespace-example&quot;).
then().
         body(&quot;foo.bar.text()&quot;, equalTo(&quot;sudo make me a sandwich!&quot;)).
         body(&quot;:foo.:bar.text()&quot;, equalTo(&quot;sudo &quot;)).
         body(&quot;foo.test:bar.text()&quot;, equalTo(&quot;make me a sandwich!&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">xmlConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">xmlConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">declareNamespace</span>(<span class="pl-s">"test"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"http://localhost/"</span>))).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/namespace-example"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"foo.bar.text()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"sudo make me a sandwich!"</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">":foo.:bar.text()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"sudo "</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"foo.test:bar.text()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"make me a sandwich!"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>The path syntax follows Groovy's XmlSlurper syntax. Note that in versions prior to 2.6.0 the path syntax was <em>not</em> following Groovy's XmlSlurper syntax. Please see <a href="https://github.com/rest-assured/rest-assured/wiki/ReleaseNotes26#non-backward-compatible-changes">release notes</a> for versin 2.6.0 to see how the previous syntax looked like.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">XPath</h3><a id="user-content-xpath" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XPath" href="#xpath"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also verify XML responses using x-path. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().parameters(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;, &quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).when().post(&quot;/greetXML&quot;).then().body(hasXPath(&quot;/greeting/firstName&quot;, containsString(&quot;Jo&quot;)));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">parameters</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">hasXPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greeting/firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">containsString</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Jo"</span>)));</pre></div>
<p>or</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().parameters(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;, &quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).post(&quot;/greetXML&quot;).then().body(hasXPath(&quot;/greeting/firstName[text()='John']&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">parameters</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).<span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">hasXPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greeting/firstName[text()='John']"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>To use namespaces in the XPath expression you need to enable them in the configuration, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(RestAssured.config().xmlConfig(xmlConfig().with().namespaceAware(true))).
when().
         get(&quot;/package-db-xml&quot;).
then().
         body(hasXPath(&quot;/db:package-database&quot;, namespaceContext));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">xmlConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">xmlConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">namespaceAware</span>(<span class="pl-c1">true</span>))).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/package-db-xml"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">hasXPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/db:package-database"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">namespaceContext</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Where <code>namespaceContext</code> is an instance of <a href="http://docs.oracle.com/javase/7/docs/api/javax/xml/namespace/NamespaceContext.html" rel="nofollow">javax.xml.namespace.NamespaceContext</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Schema and DTD validation</h3><a id="user-content-schema-and-dtd-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Schema and DTD validation" href="#schema-and-dtd-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>XML response bodies can also be verified against an XML Schema (XSD) or DTD.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">XSD example</h4><a id="user-content-xsd-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XSD example" href="#xsd-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/carRecords&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesXsd(xsd));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/carRecords"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesXsd</span>(<span class="pl-s1">xsd</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">DTD example</h4><a id="user-content-dtd-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: DTD example" href="#dtd-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/videos&quot;).then().assertThat().body(matchesDtd(dtd));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/videos"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">matchesDtd</span>(<span class="pl-s1">dtd</span>));</pre></div>
<p>The <code>matchesXsd</code> and <code>matchesDtd</code> methods are Hamcrest matchers which you can import from <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/matcher/RestAssuredMatchers.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.matcher.RestAssuredMatchers</a>.<br></p>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Example 3 - Complex parsing and validation</h2><a id="user-content-example-3---complex-parsing-and-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 3 - Complex parsing and validation" href="#example-3---complex-parsing-and-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>This is where REST Assured really starts to shine! Since REST Assured's JsonPath and XmlPath use Groovy's GPath, it can be really beneficial to take advantage of Groovy’s collection API. Let’s begin by looking at an example in Groovy:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-groovy notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="def words = ['ant', 'buffalo', 'cat', 'dinosaur']
def wordsWithSizeGreaterThanFour = words.findAll { it.length() &gt; 4 }"><pre><span class="pl-k">def</span> words <span class="pl-k">=</span> [<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>ant<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>buffalo<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>cat<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>dinosaur<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>]
<span class="pl-k">def</span> wordsWithSizeGreaterThanFour <span class="pl-k">=</span> words<span class="pl-k">.</span>findAll { it<span class="pl-k">.</span>length() <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">4</span> }</pre></div>
<p>At the first line we simply define a list with some words but the second line is more interesting.
Here we search the words list for all words that are longer than 4 characters by calling the findAll with a Groovy closure.
The closure has an implicit variable called <code>it</code> which represents the current item in the list.
The result is a new list, <code>wordsWithSizeGreaterThanFour</code>, containing <code>buffalo</code> and <code>dinosaur</code>.</p>
<p>There are other interesting methods that we can use on collections in Groovy as well, for example:</p>
<ul>
<li>
<code>find</code> – finds the first item matching a closure predicate</li>
<li>
<code>collect</code> – collect the return value of calling a closure on each item in a collection</li>
<li>
<code>sum</code> – Sum all the items in the collection</li>
<li>
<code>max</code>/<code>min</code> – returns the max/min values of the collection</li>
</ul>
<p>So how do we take advantage of this when validating our XML or JSON responses with REST Assured?</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">XML Example</h3><a id="user-content-xml-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XML Example" href="#xml-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Let’s say we have a resource at <code>http://localhost:8080/shopping</code> that returns the following XML:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;shopping&gt;
      &lt;category type=&quot;groceries&quot;&gt;
        &lt;item&gt;Chocolate&lt;/item&gt;
        &lt;item&gt;Coffee&lt;/item&gt;
      &lt;/category&gt;
      &lt;category type=&quot;supplies&quot;&gt;
        &lt;item&gt;Paper&lt;/item&gt;
        &lt;item quantity=&quot;4&quot;&gt;Pens&lt;/item&gt;
      &lt;/category&gt;
      &lt;category type=&quot;present&quot;&gt;
        &lt;item when=&quot;Aug 10&quot;&gt;Kathryn's Birthday&lt;/item&gt;
      &lt;/category&gt;
&lt;/shopping&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">shopping</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">category</span> <span class="pl-e">type</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>groceries<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;Chocolate&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;Coffee&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;
      &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">category</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">category</span> <span class="pl-e">type</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>supplies<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;Paper&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">item</span> <span class="pl-e">quantity</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>4<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>&gt;Pens&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;
      &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">category</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">category</span> <span class="pl-e">type</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>present<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">item</span> <span class="pl-e">when</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Aug 10<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>&gt;Kathryn's Birthday&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">item</span>&gt;
      &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">category</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">shopping</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p>Let’s also say we want to write a test that verifies that the category of type groceries has items Chocolate and Coffee. In REST Assured it can look like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
       get(&quot;/shopping&quot;).
then().
       body(&quot;shopping.category.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }.item&quot;, hasItems(&quot;Chocolate&quot;, &quot;Coffee&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/shopping"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"shopping.category.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }.item"</span>, <span class="pl-en">hasItems</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Chocolate"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Coffee"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>What's going on here? First of all the XML path <code>shopping.category</code> returns a list of all categories.
On this list we invoke a function, <code>find</code>, to return the single category that has the XML attribute, <code>type</code>, equal to <code>groceries</code>.
On this category we then continue by getting all the items associated with this category.
Since there are more than one item associated with the groceries category a list will be returned and we verify this list against the <code>hasItems</code> Hamcrest matcher.</p>
<p>But what if you want to get the items and not validate them against a Hamcrest matcher? For this purpose you can use <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/xml-path/6.3.0/io/restassured/path/xml/XmlPath.html" rel="nofollow">XmlPath</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Get the response body as a String
String response = get(&quot;/shopping&quot;).asString();
// And get the groceries from the response. &quot;from&quot; is statically imported from the XmlPath class
List&lt;String&gt; groceries = from(response).getList(&quot;shopping.category.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }.item&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Get the response body as a String</span>
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/shopping"</span>).<span class="pl-en">asString</span>();
<span class="pl-c">// And get the groceries from the response. "from" is statically imported from the XmlPath class</span>
<span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>&gt; <span class="pl-s1">groceries</span> = <span class="pl-en">from</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>).<span class="pl-en">getList</span>(<span class="pl-s">"shopping.category.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }.item"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>If the list of groceries is the only thing you care about in the response body you can also use a <a href="#single-path">shortcut</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Get the response body as a String
List&lt;String&gt; groceries = get(&quot;/shopping&quot;).path(&quot;shopping.category.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }.item&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Get the response body as a String</span>
<span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>&gt; <span class="pl-s1">groceries</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/shopping"</span>).<span class="pl-en">path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"shopping.category.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }.item"</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">Depth-first search</h4><a id="user-content-depth-first-search" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Depth-first search" href="#depth-first-search"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>It's actually possible to simplify the previous example even further:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
       get(&quot;/shopping&quot;).
then().
       body(&quot;**.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }&quot;, hasItems(&quot;Chocolate&quot;, &quot;Coffee&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/shopping"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"**.find { it.@type == 'groceries' }"</span>, <span class="pl-en">hasItems</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Chocolate"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Coffee"</span>));</pre></div>
<p><code>**</code> is a shortcut for doing depth first searching in the XML document.
We search for the first node that has an attribute named <code>type</code> equal to "groceries". Notice also that we don't end the XML path with "item".
The reason is that <code>toString()</code> is called automatically on the category node which returns a list of the item values.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">JSON Example</h3><a id="user-content-json-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JSON Example" href="#json-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Let's say we have a resource at <code>http://localhost:8080/store</code> that returns the following JSON document:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{  
   &quot;store&quot;:{  
      &quot;book&quot;:[  
         {  
            &quot;author&quot;:&quot;Nigel Rees&quot;,
            &quot;category&quot;:&quot;reference&quot;,
            &quot;price&quot;:8.95,
            &quot;title&quot;:&quot;Sayings of the Century&quot;
         },
         {  
            &quot;author&quot;:&quot;Evelyn Waugh&quot;,
            &quot;category&quot;:&quot;fiction&quot;,
            &quot;price&quot;:12.99,
            &quot;title&quot;:&quot;Sword of Honour&quot;
         },
         {  
            &quot;author&quot;:&quot;Herman Melville&quot;,
            &quot;category&quot;:&quot;fiction&quot;,
            &quot;isbn&quot;:&quot;0-553-21311-3&quot;,
            &quot;price&quot;:8.99,
            &quot;title&quot;:&quot;Moby Dick&quot;
         },
         {  
            &quot;author&quot;:&quot;J. R. R. Tolkien&quot;,
            &quot;category&quot;:&quot;fiction&quot;,
            &quot;isbn&quot;:&quot;0-395-19395-8&quot;,
            &quot;price&quot;:22.99,
            &quot;title&quot;:&quot;The Lord of the Rings&quot;
         }
      ]
   }
}"><pre><span class="pl-kos">{</span>  
   <span class="pl-s">"store"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">{</span>  
      <span class="pl-s">"book"</span>:<span class="pl-kos">[</span>  
         <span class="pl-kos">{</span>  
            <span class="pl-s">"author"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"Nigel Rees"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"category"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"reference"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">8.95</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"Sayings of the Century"</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">{</span>  
            <span class="pl-s">"author"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"Evelyn Waugh"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"category"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"fiction"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">12.99</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"Sword of Honour"</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">{</span>  
            <span class="pl-s">"author"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"Herman Melville"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"category"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"fiction"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"isbn"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"0-553-21311-3"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">8.99</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"Moby Dick"</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">{</span>  
            <span class="pl-s">"author"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"J. R. R. Tolkien"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"category"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"fiction"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"isbn"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"0-395-19395-8"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">22.99</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>:<span class="pl-s">"The Lord of the Rings"</span>
         <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">]</span>
   <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">Example 1</h4><a id="user-content-example-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example 1" href="#example-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As a first example let's say we want to make the request to "/store" and assert that the titles of the books with a price less than 10 are "Sayings of the Century" and "Moby Dick":</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
       get(&quot;/store&quot;).
then().
       body(&quot;store.book.findAll { it.price &lt; 10 }.title&quot;, hasItems(&quot;Sayings of the Century&quot;, &quot;Moby Dick&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/store"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"store.book.findAll { it.price &lt; 10 }.title"</span>, <span class="pl-en">hasItems</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Sayings of the Century"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Moby Dick"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Just as in the XML examples above we use a closure to find all books with a price less than 10 and then return the titles of all the books.
We then use the <code>hasItems</code> matcher to assert that the titles are the ones we expect. Using <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/json-path/6.3.0/io/restassured/path/json/JsonPath.html" rel="nofollow">JsonPath</a> we can return the titles instead:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Get the response body as a String
String response = get(&quot;/store&quot;).asString();
// And get all books with price &lt; 10 from the response. &quot;from&quot; is statically imported from the JsonPath class
