<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Filters</h1><a id="user-content-filters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Filters" href="#filters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>A filter allows you to inspect and alter a request before it's actually committed and also inspect and <a href="#response-builder">alter</a> the response before it's returned to the expectations. You can regard it as an "around advice" in AOP terms. Filters can be used to implement custom authentication schemes, session management, logging etc. To create a filter you need to implement the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/filter/Filter.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.filter.Filter</a> interface. To use a filter you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().filter(new MyFilter()). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">filter</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">MyFilter</span>()). ..</pre></div>
<p>There are a couple of filters provided by REST Assured that are ready to use:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<code>io.restassured.filter.log.RequestLoggingFilter</code>: A filter that'll print the request specification details.</li>
<li>
<code>io.restassured.filter.log.ResponseLoggingFilter</code>: A filter that'll print the response details if the response matches a given status code.</li>
<li>
<code>io.restassured.filter.log.ErrorLoggingFilter</code>: A filter that'll print the response body if an error occurred (status code is between 400 and 500).</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Ordered Filters</h3><a id="user-content-ordered-filters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ordered Filters" href="#ordered-filters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As of REST Assured 3.0.2 you can implement the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/filter/OrderedFilter.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.filter.OrderedFilter</a> interface if you need to control the filter ordering. Here you implement the <code>getOrder</code> method to return an integer representing the precedence of the filter. A lower value gives higher precedence. The highest precedence you can define is <code>Integer.MIN_VALUE</code> and the lowest precedence is <code>Integer.MAX_VALUE</code>. Filters not implementing <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/filter/OrderedFilter.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.filter.OrderedFilter</a> will have a default precedence of <code>1000</code>. Click <a href="https://github.com/rest-assured/rest-assured/blob/master/examples/rest-assured-itest-java/src/test/java/io/restassured/itest/java/OrderedFilterITest.java">here</a> for some examples.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Response Builder</h3><a id="user-content-response-builder" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Response Builder" href="#response-builder"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>If you need to change the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/response/Response.html" rel="nofollow">Response</a> from a filter you can use the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/builder/ResponseBuilder.html" rel="nofollow">ResponseBuilder</a> to create a new Response based on the original response. For example if you want to change the body of the original response to something else you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Response newResponse = new ResponseBuilder().clone(originalResponse).setBody(&quot;Something&quot;).build();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">Response</span> <span class="pl-s1">newResponse</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">ResponseBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">clone</span>(<span class="pl-s1">originalResponse</span>).<span class="pl-en">setBody</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Something"</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Logging</h1><a id="user-content-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Logging" href="#logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>In many cases it can be useful to print the response and/or request details in order to help you create the correct expectations and send the correct requests. To do help you do this you can use one of the predefined <a href="#filters">filters</a> supplied with REST Assured or you can use one of the shortcuts.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Request Logging</h2><a id="user-content-request-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Request Logging" href="#request-logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Since version 1.5 REST Assured supports logging the <em><a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/specification/RequestSpecification.html" rel="nofollow">request specification</a></em> before it's sent to the server using the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/filter/log/RequestLoggingFilter.html" rel="nofollow">RequestLoggingFilter</a>. Note that the HTTP Builder and HTTP Client may add additional headers then what's printed in the log. The filter will <em>only</em> log details specified in the request specification. I.e. you can NOT regard the details logged by the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/filter/log/RequestLoggingFilter.html" rel="nofollow">RequestLoggingFilter</a> to be what's actually sent to the server. Also subsequent filters may alter the request <em>after</em> the logging has taken place. If you need to log what's <em>actually</em> sent on the wire refer to the <a href="http://hc.apache.org/httpcomponents-client-ga/logging.html" rel="nofollow">HTTP Client logging docs</a> or use an external tool such <a href="http://www.wireshark.org/" rel="nofollow">Wireshark</a>. Examples:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().log().all(). .. // Log all request specification details including parameters, headers and body
given().log().params(). .. // Log only the parameters of the request
given().log().body(). .. // Log only the request body
given().log().headers(). .. // Log only the request headers
given().log().cookies(). .. // Log only the request cookies
given().log().method(). .. // Log only the request method
given().log().path(). .. // Log only the request path"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">all</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log all request specification details including parameters, headers and body</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">params</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log only the parameters of the request</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log only the request body</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">headers</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log only the request headers</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookies</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log only the request cookies</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">method</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log only the request method</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">path</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Log only the request path</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Response Logging</h2><a id="user-content-response-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Response Logging" href="#response-logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>If you want to print the response body regardless of the status code you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().body() .."><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>() ..</pre></div>
<p>This will print the response body regardless if an error occurred. If you're only interested in printing the response body if an error occur then you can use:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().ifError(). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">ifError</span>(). .. </pre></div>
<p>You can also log all details in the response including status line, headers and cookies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().all(). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">all</span>(). .. </pre></div>
<p>as well as only status line, headers or cookies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().statusLine(). .. // Only log the status line
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().headers(). .. // Only log the response headers
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().cookies(). .. // Only log the response cookies"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusLine</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Only log the status line</span>
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">headers</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Only log the response headers</span>
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookies</span>(). .. <span class="pl-c">// Only log the response cookies</span></pre></div>
<p>You can also configure to log the response only if the status code matches some value:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().ifStatusCodeIsEqualTo(302). .. // Only log if the status code is equal to 302
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().log().ifStatusCodeMatches(matcher). .. // Only log if the status code matches the supplied Hamcrest matcher"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">ifStatusCodeIsEqualTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">302</span>). .. <span class="pl-c">// Only log if the status code is equal to 302</span>
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">ifStatusCodeMatches</span>(<span class="pl-s1">matcher</span>). .. <span class="pl-c">// Only log if the status code matches the supplied Hamcrest matcher</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Log if validation fails</h2><a id="user-content-log-if-validation-fails" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Log if validation fails" href="#log-if-validation-fails"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Since REST Assured 2.3.1 you can log the request or response only if the validation fails. To log the request do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().log().ifValidationFails(). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">ifValidationFails</span>(). ..</pre></div>
<p>To log the response do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=".. .then().log().ifValidationFails(). .."><pre>.. .<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">log</span>().<span class="pl-en">ifValidationFails</span>(). ..</pre></div>
<p>It's also possible to enable this for both the request and the response at the same time using the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/LogConfig.html" rel="nofollow">LogConfig</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(RestAssured.config().logConfig(logConfig().enableLoggingOfRequestAndResponseIfValidationFails(HEADERS))). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">logConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">logConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">enableLoggingOfRequestAndResponseIfValidationFails</span>(<span class="pl-c1">HEADERS</span>))). ..</pre></div>
<p>This will log only the headers if validation fails.</p>
<p>There's also a shortcut for enabling logging of the request and response for all requests if validation fails:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.enableLoggingOfRequestAndResponseIfValidationFails();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">enableLoggingOfRequestAndResponseIfValidationFails</span>();</pre></div>
<p>As of version 4.5.0 you can also use the <code>onFailMessage</code> to specify a message that'll been shown when a test fails. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
      get().
then().
      onFailMessage(&quot;Some specific message&quot;).
      statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
      <span class="pl-en">get</span>().
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
      <span class="pl-en">onFailMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Some specific message"</span>).
      <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Now "Some specific message" will be shown in the error message. This is good if you want to e.g. distinguish more easily between tests if they fail.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Blacklist Headers from Logging</h2><a id="user-content-blacklist-headers-from-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Blacklist Headers from Logging" href="#blacklist-headers-from-logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As of REST Assured 4.2.0 it's possible to blacklist headers so that they are not shown in the request or response log. Instead the header value will be replaced with <code>[ BLACKLISTED ]</code>. You can enable this per header basis using the <a href="https://www.javadoc.io/doc/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/latest/io/restassured/config/LogConfig.html" rel="nofollow">LogConfig</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(config().logConfig(logConfig().blacklistHeader(&quot;Accept&quot;))). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">logConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">logConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">blacklistHeader</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Accept"</span>))). ..</pre></div>
<p>The response log will the print:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Request method:   GET
Request URI:    http://localhost:8080/something
Proxy:          &lt;none&gt;
Request params: &lt;none&gt;
Query params:   &lt;none&gt;
Form params:    &lt;none&gt;
Path params:    &lt;none&gt;
Headers:        Accept=[ BLACKLISTED ]
Cookies:        &lt;none&gt;
Multiparts:     &lt;none&gt;
Body:           &lt;none&gt;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Request method:   GET
Request URI:    http://localhost:8080/something
Proxy:          &lt;none&gt;
Request params: &lt;none&gt;
Query params:   &lt;none&gt;
Form params:    &lt;none&gt;
Path params:    &lt;none&gt;
Headers:        Accept=[ BLACKLISTED ]
Cookies:        &lt;none&gt;
Multiparts:     &lt;none&gt;
Body:           &lt;none&gt;
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Root path</h1><a id="user-content-root-path" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Root path" href="#root-path"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>To avoid duplicated paths in body expectations you can specify a root path. E.g. instead of writing:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
         get(&quot;/something&quot;).
then().
         body(&quot;x.y.firstName&quot;, is(..)).
         body(&quot;x.y.lastName&quot;, is(..)).
         body(&quot;x.y.age&quot;, is(..)).
         body(&quot;x.y.gender&quot;, is(..));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y.firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y.lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y.age"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y.gender"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..));</pre></div>
<p>you can use a root path and do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
        get(&quot;/something&quot;).
then().
         rootPath(&quot;x.y&quot;). // You can also use the &quot;root&quot; method
         body(&quot;firstName&quot;, is(..)).
         body(&quot;lastName&quot;, is(..)).
         body(&quot;age&quot;, is(..)).
         body(&quot;gender&quot;, is(..));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">rootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y"</span>). <span class="pl-c">// You can also use the "root" method</span>
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"age"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"gender"</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(..));</pre></div>
<p>You can also set a default root path using:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.rootPath = &quot;x.y&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">rootPath</span> = <span class="pl-s">"x.y"</span>;</pre></div>
<p>In more advanced use cases it may also be useful to append additional root arguments to existing root arguments. To do this you can use the <code>appendRoot</code> method, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
         get(&quot;/jsonStore&quot;).
then().
         rootPath(&quot;store.%s&quot;, withArgs(&quot;book&quot;)).
         body(&quot;category.size()&quot;, equalTo(4)).
         appendRootPath(&quot;%s.%s&quot;, withArgs(&quot;author&quot;, &quot;size()&quot;)).
         body(withNoArgs(), equalTo(4));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/jsonStore"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">rootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"store.%s"</span>, <span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"book"</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"category.size()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">4</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">appendRootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"%s.%s"</span>, <span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"author"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"size()"</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">withNoArgs</span>(), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">4</span>));</pre></div>
<p>It's also possible to detach a root. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
         get(&quot;/jsonStore&quot;).
then().
         rootPath(&quot;store.category&quot;).
         body(&quot;size()&quot;, equalTo(4)).
         detachRootPath(&quot;category&quot;).
         body(&quot;size()&quot;, equalTo(1));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/jsonStore"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">rootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"store.category"</span>).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"size()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">4</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">detachRootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"category"</span>).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"size()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Path arguments</h1><a id="user-content-path-arguments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Path arguments" href="#path-arguments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Path arguments are useful in situations where you have e.g. pre-defined variables that constitutes the path. For example</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String someSubPath = &quot;else&quot;;
int index = 1;
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().body(&quot;something.%s[%d]&quot;, withArgs(someSubPath, index), equalTo(&quot;some value&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">someSubPath</span> = <span class="pl-s">"else"</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">index</span> = <span class="pl-c1">1</span>;
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"something.%s[%d]"</span>, <span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-s1">someSubPath</span>, <span class="pl-s1">index</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some value"</span>)). ..</pre></div>
<p>will expect that the body path "<code>something.else[0]</code>" is equal to "some value".</p>
<p>Another usage is if you have complex <a href="http://code.google.com/p/rest-assured/wiki/Usage#Root_path" rel="nofollow">root paths</a> and don't wish to duplicate the path for small variations:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
       get(&quot;/x&quot;).
then().
       rootPath(&quot;filters.filterConfig[%d].filterConfigGroups.find { it.name == 'GroupName' }.includes&quot;).
       body(withArgs(0), hasItem(&quot;first&quot;)).
       body(withArgs(1), hasItem(&quot;second&quot;)).
       .."><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">rootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"filters.filterConfig[%d].filterConfigGroups.find { it.name == 'GroupName' }.includes"</span>).
       <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0</span>), <span class="pl-en">hasItem</span>(<span class="pl-s">"first"</span>)).
       <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>), <span class="pl-en">hasItem</span>(<span class="pl-s">"second"</span>)).
       ..</pre></div>
<p>The path arguments follows the standard <a href="http://download.oracle.com/javase/1,5.0/docs/api/java/util/Formatter.html#syntax" rel="nofollow">formatting syntax</a> of Java.</p>
<p>Note that the <code>withArgs</code> method can be statically imported from the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/RestAssured.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.RestAssured</a> class.</p>
<p>Sometimes it's also useful to validate a body without any additional arguments when all arguments have already been specified in the root path. This is where <code>withNoArgs</code> come into play. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
         get(&quot;/jsonStore&quot;).
then().
         rootPath(&quot;store.%s&quot;, withArgs(&quot;book&quot;)).
         body(&quot;category.size()&quot;, equalTo(4)).
         appendRootPath(&quot;%s.%s&quot;, withArgs(&quot;author&quot;, &quot;size()&quot;)).
         body(withNoArgs(), equalTo(4));"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/jsonStore"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         <span class="pl-en">rootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"store.%s"</span>, <span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"book"</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"category.size()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">4</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">appendRootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"%s.%s"</span>, <span class="pl-en">withArgs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"author"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"size()"</span>)).
         <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">withNoArgs</span>(), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">4</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Session support</h1><a id="user-content-session-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Session support" href="#session-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>REST Assured provides a simplified way for managing sessions. You can define a session id value in the DSL:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().sessionId(&quot;1234&quot;). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">sessionId</span>(<span class="pl-s">"1234"</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>This is actually just a short-cut for:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().cookie(&quot;JSESSIONID&quot;, &quot;1234&quot;). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookie</span>(<span class="pl-s">"JSESSIONID"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"1234"</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>You can also specify a default <code>sessionId</code> that'll be supplied with all subsequent requests:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.sessionId = &quot;1234&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">sessionId</span> = <span class="pl-s">"1234"</span>;</pre></div>
<p>The session id is applied by <code>RestAssured.config()</code>, not stored in the <code>RestAssured.config</code> field. If you pass the static config yourself and want the session id sent, use <code>given().config(RestAssured.config())</code> and not <code>given().config(RestAssured.config)</code>.</p>
<p>By default the session id name is <code>JSESSIONID</code> but you can change it using the <a href="#session-config">SessionConfig</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().sessionConfig(new SessionConfig().sessionIdName(&quot;phpsessionid&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">sessionConfig</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">SessionConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">sessionIdName</span>(<span class="pl-s">"phpsessionid"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>You can also specify a sessionId using the <code>RequestSpecBuilder</code> and reuse it in many tests:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RequestSpecBuilder spec = new RequestSpecBuilder().setSessionId(&quot;value1&quot;).build();
   
// Make the first request with session id equal to value1
given().spec(spec). .. 
// Make the second request with session id equal to value1
given().spec(spec). .. "><pre><span class="pl-smi">RequestSpecBuilder</span> <span class="pl-s1">spec</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">RequestSpecBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setSessionId</span>(<span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();
   
<span class="pl-c">// Make the first request with session id equal to value1</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">spec</span>(<span class="pl-s1">spec</span>). .. 
<span class="pl-c">// Make the second request with session id equal to value1</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">spec</span>(<span class="pl-s1">spec</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>It's also possible to get the session id from the response object:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String sessionId = get(&quot;/something&quot;).sessionId();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">sessionId</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).<span class="pl-en">sessionId</span>();</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Session Filter</h2><a id="user-content-session-filter" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Session Filter" href="#session-filter"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As of version 2.0.0 you can use a <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/filter/session/SessionFilter.html" rel="nofollow">session filter</a> to automatically capture and apply the session, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="SessionFilter sessionFilter = new SessionFilter();

given().
          auth().form(&quot;John&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).
          filter(sessionFilter).
when().
          get(&quot;/formAuth&quot;).
then().
          statusCode(200);


given().
          filter(sessionFilter). // Reuse the same session filter instance to automatically apply the session id from the previous response
when().
          get(&quot;/x&quot;).
then().
          statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">SessionFilter</span> <span class="pl-s1">sessionFilter</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">SessionFilter</span>();

<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
          <span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">form</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).
          <span class="pl-en">filter</span>(<span class="pl-s1">sessionFilter</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
          <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/formAuth"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
          <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);


<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
          <span class="pl-en">filter</span>(<span class="pl-s1">sessionFilter</span>). <span class="pl-c">// Reuse the same session filter instance to automatically apply the session id from the previous response</span>
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
          <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
          <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>To get session id caught by the <code>SessionFilter</code> you can do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String sessionId = sessionFilter.getSessionId();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">sessionId</span> = <span class="pl-s1">sessionFilter</span>.<span class="pl-en">getSessionId</span>();</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">SSL</h1><a id="user-content-ssl" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SSL" href="#ssl"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>In most situations SSL should just work out of the box thanks to the excellent work of HTTP Builder and HTTP Client. There are however some cases where you'll run into trouble. You may for example run into a SSLPeerUnverifiedException if the server is using an invalid certificate. The easiest way to workaround this is to use "relaxed HTTPs validation". For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().relaxedHTTPSValidation().when().get(&quot;https://some_server.com&quot;). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">relaxedHTTPSValidation</span>().<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"https://some_server.com"</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>You can also define this statically for all requests:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.useRelaxedHTTPSValidation();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">useRelaxedHTTPSValidation</span>();</pre></div>
<p>or in a <a href="#specification-re-use">request specification</a>.</p>
<p>Since REST Assured 6.1.0, SSL settings also apply when a request starts as plain <code>http</code> and the server redirects it to <code>https</code>. This includes relaxed HTTPS validation, key and trust stores, and certificate authentication. Before, they only applied if the first request was already <code>https</code>. For example, this works even if <code>http://localhost:9080</code> redirects to an <code>https</code> address with a self-signed certificate:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().relaxedHTTPSValidation().when().get(&quot;http://localhost:9080/app&quot;).then().statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">relaxedHTTPSValidation</span>().<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"http://localhost:9080/app"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>This will assume an SSLContext protocol of  <code>SSL</code>. To change to another protocol use an overloaded versionen of  <code>relaxedHTTPSValidation</code>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().relaxedHTTPSValidation(&quot;TLS&quot;).when().get(&quot;https://some_server.com&quot;). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">relaxedHTTPSValidation</span>(<span class="pl-s">"TLS"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"https://some_server.com"</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>You can also be more fine-grained and use your own Java key stores. To trust the server certificate, put it in a trust store and use it like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().trustStore(&quot;/pathToJksInClassPath&quot;, &lt;password&gt;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">trustStore</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/pathToJksInClassPath"</span>, &lt;<span class="pl-s1">password</span>&gt;<span class="pl-s1"></span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>or you can specify it for every request:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.trustStore(&quot;/pathToJksInClassPath&quot;, &lt;password&gt;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">trustStore</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/pathToJksInClassPath"</span>, &lt;<span class="pl-s1">password</span>&gt;<span class="pl-s1"></span>);</pre></div>
<p>If you already loaded a keystore with a password you can use it as a truststore:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.trustStore(keystore);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">trustStore</span>(<span class="pl-s1">keystore</span>);</pre></div>
<p>If the server asks for a client certificate (mutual TLS), put the client certificate and its private key in a key store and use it like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().keyStore(&quot;/pathToJksInClassPath&quot;, &lt;password&gt;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">keyStore</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/pathToJksInClassPath"</span>, &lt;<span class="pl-s1">password</span>&gt;<span class="pl-s1"></span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>or for every request:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.keyStore(&quot;/pathToJksInClassPath&quot;, &lt;password&gt;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">keyStore</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/pathToJksInClassPath"</span>, &lt;<span class="pl-s1">password</span>&gt;<span class="pl-s1"></span>);</pre></div>
<p>The key store doesn't affect which server certificates are trusted. That's decided by the trust store if you configure one, and by the JVM's default trust otherwise. The private key must be readable with the key store password. Since REST Assured 6.2.0 the key store is used even when no trust store is configured. Before, it was ignored in that case. Note that a server that only requests a client certificate, and doesn't trust it, may then reject the connection.</p>
<p>A <code>KeyStore</code> that you have already loaded can also be used as key store, with <code>given().keyStore(keyStore)</code> or <code>sslConfig().keyStore(keyStore)</code>. If its private key has a password, set it with <code>sslConfig().keyStore(keyStore).keyStore(&lt;password&gt;)</code>.</p>
<p>You can also define a trust store and a key store in a re-usable <a href="#specification-re-use">specification</a>.</p>
<p>You can find a working example <a href="https://github.com/rest-assured/rest-assured/blob/master/examples/rest-assured-itest-java/src/test/java/io/restassured/itest/java/SSLTest.java">here</a>.</p>
<p>For more advanced SSL Configuration refer to the <a href="#ssl-config">SSL Configuration</a> section.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">SSL invalid hostname</h2><a id="user-content-ssl-invalid-hostname" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SSL invalid hostname" href="#ssl-invalid-hostname"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>If the certificate is specifying an invalid hostname you don't need to create and import a keystore. As of version <code>2.2.0</code> you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().sslConfig(sslConfig().allowAllHostnames());"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">allowAllHostnames</span>());</pre></div>
<p>to allow all hostnames for all requests or:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(RestAssured.config().sslConfig(sslConfig().allowAllHostnames()). .. ;"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-s1">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">allowAllHostnames</span>()). .. ;</pre></div>
<p>for a single request.</p>
<p>Note that if you use "relaxed HTTPs validation" then <code>allowAllHostnames</code> is activated by default.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">URL Encoding</h1><a id="user-content-url-encoding" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: URL Encoding" href="#url-encoding"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Usually you don't have to think about URL encoding since Rest Assured provides this automatically out of the box. In some cases though it may be useful to turn URL Encoding off. One reason may be that you already the have some parameters encoded before you supply them to Rest Assured. To prevent double URL encoding you need to tell Rest Assured to disable it's URL encoding. E.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String response = given().urlEncodingEnabled(false).get(&quot;https://jira.atlassian.com:443/rest/api/2.0.alpha1/search?jql=project%20=%20BAM%20AND%20issuetype%20=%20Bug&quot;).asString();
.."><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span> = <span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">urlEncodingEnabled</span>(<span class="pl-c1">false</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"https://jira.atlassian.com:443/rest/api/2.0.alpha1/search?jql=project%20=%20BAM%20AND%20issuetype%20=%20Bug"</span>).<span class="pl-en">asString</span>();
..</pre></div>
<p>or</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.baseURI = &quot;https://jira.atlassian.com&quot;;
RestAssured.port = 443;
RestAssured.urlEncodingEnabled = false;
final String query = &quot;project%20=%20BAM%20AND%20issuetype%20=%20Bug&quot;;
String response = get(&quot;/rest/api/2.0.alpha1/search?jql={q}&quot;, query);
.."><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">baseURI</span> = <span class="pl-s">"https://jira.atlassian.com"</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">port</span> = <span class="pl-c1">443</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">urlEncodingEnabled</span> = <span class="pl-c1">false</span>;
<span class="pl-k">final</span> <span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">query</span> = <span class="pl-s">"project%20=%20BAM%20AND%20issuetype%20=%20Bug"</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/rest/api/2.0.alpha1/search?jql={q}"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">query</span>);
..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Proxy Configuration</h1><a id="user-content-proxy-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Proxy Configuration" href="#proxy-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Starting from version 2.3.2 REST Assured has better support for proxies. For example if you have a proxy at localhost port 8888 you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().proxy(&quot;localhost&quot;, 8888). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">proxy</span>(<span class="pl-s">"localhost"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">8888</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>Actually you don't even have to specify the hostname if the server is running on your local environment:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().proxy(8888). .. // Will assume localhost"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">proxy</span>(<span class="pl-c1">8888</span>). .. <span class="pl-c">// Will assume localhost</span></pre></div>
<p>To use HTTPS you need to supply a third parameter (scheme) or use the <code>io.restassured.specification.ProxySpecification</code>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().proxy(host(&quot;localhost&quot;).withScheme(&quot;https&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">proxy</span>(<span class="pl-en">host</span>(<span class="pl-s">"localhost"</span>).<span class="pl-en">withScheme</span>(<span class="pl-s">"https"</span>)). ..</pre></div>
<p>where <code>host</code> is statically imported from <code>io.restassured.specification.ProxySpecification</code>.</p>
<p>Starting from version 2.7.0 you can also specify preemptive basic authentication for proxies. For example:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().proxy(auth(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;)).when() .."><pre class="notranslate"><code>given().proxy(auth("username", "password")).when() ..
</code></pre></div>
<p>where <code>auth</code> is statically imported from <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/specification/ProxySpecification.html" rel="nofollow">io.restassured.specification.ProxySpecification</a>. You can of course also combine authentication with a different host:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().proxy(host(&quot;http://myhost.org&quot;).withAuth(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">proxy</span>(<span class="pl-en">host</span>(<span class="pl-s">"http://myhost.org"</span>).<span class="pl-en">withAuth</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>)). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Static Proxy Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-static-proxy-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Static Proxy Configuration" href="#static-proxy-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>It's also possible to configure a proxy statically for all requests, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.proxy(&quot;localhost&quot;, 8888);    "><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">proxy</span>(<span class="pl-s">"localhost"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">8888</span>);    </pre></div>
<p>or:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.proxy = host(&quot;localhost&quot;).withPort(8888);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">proxy</span> = <span class="pl-en">host</span>(<span class="pl-s">"localhost"</span>).<span class="pl-en">withPort</span>(<span class="pl-c1">8888</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Request Specification Proxy Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-request-specification-proxy-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Request Specification Proxy Configuration" href="#request-specification-proxy-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also create a request specification and specify the proxy there:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RequestSpecification specification = new RequestSpecBuilder().setProxy(&quot;localhost&quot;).build();
given().spec(specification). .."><pre><span class="pl-smi">RequestSpecification</span> <span class="pl-s1">specification</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">RequestSpecBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setProxy</span>(<span class="pl-s">"localhost"</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">spec</span>(<span class="pl-s1">specification</span>). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Detailed configuration</h1><a id="user-content-detailed-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Detailed configuration" href="#detailed-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Detailed configuration is provided by the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/RestAssuredConfig.html" rel="nofollow">RestAssuredConfig</a> instance with which you can configure the parameters of <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/HttpClientConfig.html" rel="nofollow">HTTP Client</a> as well as <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/RedirectConfig.html" rel="nofollow">Redirect</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/LogConfig.html" rel="nofollow">Log</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/EncoderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">Encoder</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/DecoderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">Decoder</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/SessionConfig.html" rel="nofollow">Session</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/ObjectMapperConfig.html" rel="nofollow">ObjectMapper</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/ConnectionConfig.html" rel="nofollow">Connection</a>, <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/SSLConfig.html" rel="nofollow">SSL</a> and <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/ParamConfig.html" rel="nofollow">ParamConfig</a> settings. Examples:</p>
<p>For a specific request:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(RestAssured.config().redirect(redirectConfig().followRedirects(false))). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">redirect</span>(<span class="pl-en">redirectConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">followRedirects</span>(<span class="pl-c1">false</span>))). ..</pre></div>
<p>or using a RequestSpecBuilder:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RequestSpecification spec = new RequestSpecBuilder().setConfig(RestAssured.config().redirect(redirectConfig().followRedirects(false))).build();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RequestSpecification</span> <span class="pl-s1">spec</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">RequestSpecBuilder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setConfig</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">redirect</span>(<span class="pl-en">redirectConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">followRedirects</span>(<span class="pl-c1">false</span>))).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();</pre></div>
<p>or for all requests:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = config().redirect(redirectConfig().followRedirects(true).and().maxRedirects(0));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">redirect</span>(<span class="pl-en">redirectConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">followRedirects</span>(<span class="pl-c1">true</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">maxRedirects</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0</span>));</pre></div>
<p><code>config()</code> and <code>newConfig()</code> can be statically imported from <code>io.restassured.config.RestAssuredConfig</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Encoder Config</h2><a id="user-content-encoder-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Encoder Config" href="#encoder-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>With the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/EncoderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">EncoderConfig</a> you can specify the default content encoding charset (if it's not specified in the content-type header) and query parameter charset for all requests. If no content charset is specified then ISO-8859-1 is used and if no query parameter charset is specified then UTF-8 is used. Usage example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().encoderConfig(encoderConfig().defaultContentCharset(&quot;US-ASCII&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">encoderConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">encoderConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">defaultContentCharset</span>(<span class="pl-s">"US-ASCII"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>You can also specify which encoder charset to use for a specific content-type if no charset is defined explicitly for this content-type by using the <code>defaultCharsetForContentType</code> method in the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/EncoderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">EncoderConfig</a>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config(config().encoderConfig(encoderConfig().defaultCharsetForContentType(&quot;UTF-16&quot;, &quot;application/xml&quot;)));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">encoderConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">encoderConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">defaultCharsetForContentType</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-16"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"application/xml"</span>)));</pre></div>
<p>This will assume UTF-16 encoding for "application/xml" content-types that does explicitly specify a charset. By default "application/json" is specified to use "UTF-8" as default content-type as this is specified by <a href="https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc4627.txt" rel="nofollow">RFC4627</a>. Before REST Assured 6.2.0 <code>defaultCharsetForContentType</code> also changed the default content charset for every other content type. Call <code>defaultContentCharset(..)</code> too if you relied on that.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Avoid adding the charset to content-type header automatically</h3><a id="user-content-avoid-adding-the-charset-to-content-type-header-automatically" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Avoid adding the charset to content-type header automatically" href="#avoid-adding-the-charset-to-content-type-header-automatically"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>By default REST Assured adds the charset header automatically. To disable this completely you can configure the <code>EncoderConfig</code> like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config(config().encoderConfig(encoderConfig().appendDefaultContentCharsetToContentTypeIfUndefined(false));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">encoderConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">encoderConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">appendDefaultContentCharsetToContentTypeIfUndefined</span>(<span class="pl-c1">false</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Decoder Config</h2><a id="user-content-decoder-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Decoder Config" href="#decoder-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>With the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/DecoderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">DecoderConfig</a> you can set the default response content decoding charset for all responses. This is useful if you expect a different content charset than ISO-8859-1 (which is the default charset) and the response doesn't define the charset in the content-type header. Usage example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().decoderConfig(decoderConfig().defaultContentCharset(&quot;UTF-8&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">decoderConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">decoderConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">defaultContentCharset</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-8"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>You can also use the <code>DecoderConfig</code> to specify which content decoders to apply. When you do this the <code>Accept-Encoding</code> header will be added automatically to the request and the response body will be decoded automatically. By default GZIP and DEFLATE decoders are enabled. To for example to remove GZIP decoding but retain DEFLATE decoding you can do the following:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(RestAssured.config().decoderConfig(decoderConfig().contentDecoders(DEFLATE))). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">decoderConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">decoderConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">contentDecoders</span>(<span class="pl-c1">DEFLATE</span>))). ..</pre></div>
<p>You can also specify which decoder charset to use for a specific content-type if no charset is defined explicitly for this content-type by using the "defaultCharsetForContentType" method in the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/DecoderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">DecoderConfig</a>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = config(config().decoderConfig(decoderConfig().defaultCharsetForContentType(&quot;UTF-16&quot;, &quot;application/xml&quot;)));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">decoderConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">decoderConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">defaultCharsetForContentType</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-16"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"application/xml"</span>)));</pre></div>
<p>This will assume UTF-16 encoding for "application/xml" content-types that does explicitly specify a charset. By default all JSON content-types use UTF-8 when the response doesn't specify a charset, as <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8259" rel="nofollow">RFC 8259</a> requires. This covers <code>application/json</code>, <code>text/json</code>, <code>application/json</code> with extra parameters (such as <code>application/json; version=1</code>), and since REST Assured 6.1.0 every <code>+json</code> type, such as <code>application/problem+json</code> or <code>application/hal+json</code>. A charset you configure with <code>defaultCharsetForContentType</code> takes precedence. Before REST Assured 6.2.0 <code>defaultCharsetForContentType</code> also changed the default content charset for every other content type. Call <code>defaultContentCharset(..)</code> too if you relied on that.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Session Config</h2><a id="user-content-session-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Session Config" href="#session-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>With the session config you can configure the default session id name that's used by REST Assured. The default session id name is <code>JSESSIONID</code> and you only need to change it if the name in your application is different and you want to make use of REST Assured's <a href="#Session_support">session support</a>. Usage:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().sessionConfig(new SessionConfig().sessionIdName(&quot;phpsessionid&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">sessionConfig</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">SessionConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">sessionIdName</span>(<span class="pl-s">"phpsessionid"</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Redirect DSL</h2><a id="user-content-redirect-dsl" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Redirect DSL" href="#redirect-dsl"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Redirect configuration can also be specified using the DSL. E.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().redirects().max(12).and().redirects().follow(true).when(). ..
