"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">redirects</span>().<span class="pl-en">max</span>(<span class="pl-c1">12</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">redirects</span>().<span class="pl-en">follow</span>(<span class="pl-c1">true</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>(). .. </pre></div>
<p>By default only redirects of GET and HEAD requests are followed. Since REST Assured 6.3.0 you can follow redirects of POST, PUT, DELETE and other requests too:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().redirect(redirectConfig().followRedirectsForAllMethods(true));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">redirect</span>(<span class="pl-en">redirectConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">followRedirectsForAllMethods</span>(<span class="pl-c1">true</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Connection Config</h2><a id="user-content-connection-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Connection Config" href="#connection-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Lets you configure connection settings for REST Assured. For example if you want to force-close the Apache HTTP Client connection after each response. You may want to do this if you make a lot of fast consecutive requests with small amount of data in the response. However if you're downloading (especially large amounts of) chunked data you must not close connections after each response. By default connections are <em>not</em> closed after each response.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().connectionConfig(connectionConfig().closeIdleConnectionsAfterEachResponse());"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">connectionConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">connectionConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">closeIdleConnectionsAfterEachResponse</span>());</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Json Config</h2><a id="user-content-json-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Json Config" href="#json-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p><a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/path/json/config/JsonPathConfig.html" rel="nofollow">JsonPathConfig</a> allows you to configure the Json settings either when used by REST Assured or by <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/path/json/JsonPath.html" rel="nofollow">JsonPath</a>. It let's you configure how JSON numbers should be treated.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().jsonConfig(jsonConfig().numberReturnType(NumberReturnType.BIG_DECIMAL))"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">jsonConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">jsonConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">numberReturnType</span>(<span class="pl-smi">NumberReturnType</span>.<span class="pl-c1">BIG_DECIMAL</span>))</pre></div>
<p>Import <code>NumberReturnType</code> from <code>io.restassured.common.config</code>. <code>JsonPathConfig.NumberReturnType</code> is deprecated since REST Assured 6.3.0.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">HTTP Client Config</h2><a id="user-content-http-client-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: HTTP Client Config" href="#http-client-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Let's you configure properties for the HTTP Client instance that REST Assured will be using when executing requests. By default REST Assured creates a new instance of http client for each "given" statement. To configure reuse do the following:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().httpClient(httpClientConfig().reuseHttpClientInstance());"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">httpClient</span>(<span class="pl-en">httpClientConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">reuseHttpClientInstance</span>());</pre></div>
<p>The reused HTTP client is reconfigured by every request, so don't use it for requests that run at the same time, for example in parallel tests.</p>
<p>Since REST Assured 6.3.0 you can set timeouts and other HTTP settings directly:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().httpClient(httpClientConfig()
        .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
        .readTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
        .connectionRequestTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2)));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">httpClient</span>(<span class="pl-en">httpClientConfig</span>()
        .<span class="pl-en">connectTimeout</span>(<span class="pl-smi">Duration</span>.<span class="pl-en">ofSeconds</span>(<span class="pl-c1">5</span>))
        .<span class="pl-en">readTimeout</span>(<span class="pl-smi">Duration</span>.<span class="pl-en">ofSeconds</span>(<span class="pl-c1">10</span>))
        .<span class="pl-en">connectionRequestTimeout</span>(<span class="pl-smi">Duration</span>.<span class="pl-en">ofSeconds</span>(<span class="pl-c1">2</span>)));</pre></div>
<p><code>keepResponseCookies(true)</code> sends the cookies a response sets in the requests that follow it, for example when a redirect is followed (by default they're not kept). <code>cookieDatePatterns(..)</code> sets the date formats for reading a non-standard <code>expires</code> attribute of those cookies, <code>expectContinue(true)</code> sends <code>Expect: 100-continue</code>, and <code>multipartMode(..)</code> sets how multipart requests are encoded.</p>
<p>These replace the HttpClient 4 parameters, such as <code>setParam(CoreConnectionPNames.CONNECTION_TIMEOUT, 5000)</code>, which are deprecated. <code>httpClientFactory(..)</code>, which supplies your own HttpClient 4 instance, is deprecated too, since REST Assured 7.0.0 no longer uses HttpClient 4. See <a href="PreparingFor70#httpclient-4-parameters">Preparing for REST Assured 7.0</a> for the full list.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">SSL Config</h2><a id="user-content-ssl-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SSL Config" href="#ssl-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/SSLConfig.html" rel="nofollow">SSLConfig</a> allows you to specify more advanced SSL configuration such as truststore, keystore type and host name verifier. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().sslConfig(sslConfig().with().keystoreType(&lt;type&gt;).and().strictHostnames());"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">keystoreType</span>(&lt;<span class="pl-s1">type</span>&gt;).<span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">strictHostnames</span>());</pre></div>
<p>Since REST Assured 6.3.0 you can also give it a <code>javax.net.ssl.SSLContext</code> and a <code>javax.net.ssl.HostnameVerifier</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.config = RestAssured.config().sslConfig(sslConfig().sslContext(sslContext).hostnameVerifier(hostnameVerifier));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">sslConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">sslContext</span>(<span class="pl-s1">sslContext</span>).<span class="pl-en">hostnameVerifier</span>(<span class="pl-s1">hostnameVerifier</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Host names are still verified strictly by default when you set an <code>SSLContext</code>. <code>sslSocketFactory(..)</code> and <code>x509HostnameVerifier(..)</code>, which take Apache HttpClient 4 types, are deprecated.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Param Config</h2><a id="user-content-param-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Param Config" href="#param-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p><a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/ParamConfig.html" rel="nofollow">ParamConfig</a> allows you to configure how different parameter types should be updated on "collision". By default all parameters are merged so if you do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().queryParam(&quot;param1&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;).queryParam(&quot;param1&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;).when().get(&quot;/x&quot;). ..."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">queryParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>).<span class="pl-en">queryParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>). ...</pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will send a query string of <code>param1=value1&amp;param1=value2</code>. This is not always what you want though so you can configure REST Assured to <em>replace</em> values instead:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(config().paramConfig(paramConfig().queryParamsUpdateStrategy(REPLACE))).
        queryParam(&quot;param1&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;).
        queryParam(&quot;param1&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/x&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">paramConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">paramConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">queryParamsUpdateStrategy</span>(<span class="pl-c1">REPLACE</span>))).
        <span class="pl-en">queryParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">queryParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will now replace <code>param1</code> with <code>value2</code> (since it's written last) instead of merging them together. You can also configure the update strategy for each type of for all parameter types instead of doing it per individual basis:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(config().paramConfig(paramConfig().replaceAllParameters())). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">paramConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">paramConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">replaceAllParameters</span>())). ..</pre></div>
<p>This is also supported in the <a href="#spring-mock-mvc-module">Spring Mock Mvc Module</a> (but the config there is called <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/spring-mock-mvc/6.3.0/io/restassured/module/mockmvc/config/MockMvcParamConfig.html" rel="nofollow">MockMvcParamConfig</a>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Failure Config</h2><a id="user-content-failure-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Failure Config" href="#failure-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Added in version 3.3.0 the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/FailureConfig.html" rel="nofollow">FailureConfig</a> can be used to get callbacks when REST Assured validation fails. This is useful if you want to do some custom logging or store data available in the request/response specification or in the response itself somewhere. For example let's say that you want to be notified by email when the following test case fails because the status code is not 200:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
    param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;)
when().
    get(&quot;/hello&quot;)
then().
    statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>)
<span class="pl-k">when</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/hello"</span>)
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>You can then implement a <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/listener/ResponseValidationFailureListener.html" rel="nofollow">ResponseValidationFailureListener</a> and add it to the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/FailureConfig.html" rel="nofollow">FailureConfig</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ResponseValidationFailureListener emailOnFailure = (reqSpec, respSpec, resp) -&gt; emailService.sendEmail(&quot;email@gmail.com&quot;, &quot;Important test failed! Status code was: &quot; + resp.statusCode());

given().
    config(RestAssured.config().failureConfig(failureConfig().with().failureListeners(emailOnFailure))).
    param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;)
when().
    get(&quot;/hello&quot;)
then().
    statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">ResponseValidationFailureListener</span> <span class="pl-s1">emailOnFailure</span> = (<span class="pl-s1">reqSpec</span>, <span class="pl-s1">respSpec</span>, <span class="pl-s1">resp</span>) -&gt; <span class="pl-s1">emailService</span>.<span class="pl-en">sendEmail</span>(<span class="pl-s">"email@gmail.com"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Important test failed! Status code was: "</span> + <span class="pl-s1">resp</span>.<span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>());

<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">failureConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">failureConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">failureListeners</span>(<span class="pl-s1">emailOnFailure</span>))).
    <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>)
<span class="pl-k">when</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/hello"</span>)
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">CSRF Config</h2><a id="user-content-csrf-config" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF Config" href="#csrf-config"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Added in version 5.2.0 the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/config/CsrfConfig.html" rel="nofollow">CsrfConfig</a> can be used to configure REST Assured to support CSRF. It provides more fine-grained control that is not available in the normal <a href="#csrf">DSL</a>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
    config(config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().with().csrfTokenPath(&quot;/loginPageWithCsrf&quot;).and().loggingEnabled(LogDetail.BODY))).
when().
    post(&quot;/loginPageWithCsrf&quot;).
then().
    statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">csrfConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">csrfConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">with</span>().<span class="pl-en">csrfTokenPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/loginPageWithCsrf"</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">loggingEnabled</span>(<span class="pl-smi">LogDetail</span>.<span class="pl-c1">BODY</span>))).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/loginPageWithCsrf"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">More info</h1><a id="user-content-more-info" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: More info" href="#more-info"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>For more information refer to the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/index.html" rel="nofollow">javadoc</a>:</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/RestAssured.html" rel="nofollow">RestAssured</a></li>
<li><a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/spring-mock-mvc/6.3.0/io/restassured/module/mockmvc/RestAssuredMockMvc.html" rel="nofollow">RestAssuredMockMvc Javadoc</a></li>
<li><a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.3.0/io/restassured/specification/package-summary.html" rel="nofollow">Specification package</a></li>
</ul>
<p>You can also have a look at some code examples:</p>
<ul>
<li>REST Assured <a href="https://github.com/rest-assured/rest-assured/tree/master/examples/rest-assured-itest-java/src/test/java/io/restassured/itest/java">tests</a>
</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/rest-assured/rest-assured/blob/master/json-path/src/test/java/io/restassured/path/json/JsonPathTest.java">JsonPathTest</a></li>

</ul>
<p>If you need support then join the <a href="http://groups.google.com/group/rest-assured" rel="nofollow">mailing list</a>.</p>
<p>For professional support please contact <a href="https://github.com/johanhaleby">johanhaleby</a>.</p>

      <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-x">
    <path d="M3.72 3.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0L8 6.94l3.22-3.22a.749.749 0 0 1 1.275.326.749.749 0 0 1-.215.734L9.06 8l3.22 3.22a.749.749 0 0 1-.326 1.275.749.749 0 0 1-.734-.215L8 9.06l-3.22 3.22a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042L6.94 8 3.72 4.78a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06Z"></path>
</svg>
    </button>
    You can’t perform that action at this time.
  </div>

    <template id="site-details-dialog">
  <details class="details-reset details-overlay details-overlay-dark lh-default color-fg-default hx_rsm" open>
    <summary role="button" aria-label="Close dialog"></summary>
    <details-dialog class="Box Box--overlay d-flex flex-column anim-fade-in fast hx_rsm-dialog hx_rsm-modal">
      <button class="Box-btn-octicon m-0 btn-octicon position-absolute right-0 top-0" type="button" aria-label="Close dialog" data-close-dialog>
        <svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-x">
    <path d="M3.72 3.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0L8 6.94l3.22-3.22a.749.749 0 0 1 1.275.326.749.749 0 0 1-.215.734L9.06 8l3.22 3.22a.749.749 0 0 1-.326 1.275.749.749 0 0 1-.734-.215L8 9.06l-3.22 3.22a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042L6.94 8 3.72 4.78a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06Z"></path>
</svg>
      </button>
      <div class="octocat-spinner tmp-my-6 js-details-dialog-spinner"></div>
    </details-dialog>
  </details>
</template>

    <div class="Popover js-hovercard-content position-absolute" style="display: none; outline: none;">
